# Feature Inventory

This is a user-perspective inventory for QA planning. It is not a coverage report yet. The
coverage column is deliberately a placeholder until each feature is mapped to an explicit
coverage method and measurement.

Source pass date: 2026-06-24.

## How to Use This Inventory

- **Owner** separates core platform surfaces from bundled system extensions.
- **User feature** names what a user or external agent can actually do.
- **Current test hooks** lists obvious automated tests, smoke scripts, or docs found during the audit.
- **Coverage** is `TBD` until a later pass assigns a percentage from concrete assertions or coverage data.
- **QA notes** call out app-path, visual, live integration, or manual checks that should not be inferred from unit tests.
- Live app-path QA must inspect the whole composed viewport, including inactive split panes and restored workbench tabs.
  A raw stack trace in any visible panel is a finding even when the primary route/control works.
- **QA status ledger** lives in [`docs/feature-qa-status.md`](./feature-qa-status.md). Use it to track each feature as
  `Inventory only`, `Automated only`, `Live QA partial`, or `Live QA pass`.
- A feature should not be considered `Live QA pass` unless the row records the actual user-visible path exercised, the
  latest evidence date or screenshot/log artifact, the relevant focused tests/builds, and no known remaining gaps.

Primary validation command families found during the audit:

| Scope                       | Command                              |
| --------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| All Vitest tests            | `pnpm test`                          |
| Full repository check       | `pnpm run check`                     |
| Extension static gates      | `pnpm run check:extensions:static`   |
| Extension smoke tests       | `pnpm run test:extensions`           |
| Release hardening tests     | `pnpm run test:release-hardening`    |
| Release doctor              | `pnpm run check:release:doctor`      |
| Release QA checklist runner | `pnpm run qa:release`                |
| Startup smoke               | `pnpm run smoke:startup-idle`        |
| Chat E2E smoke              | `pnpm run e2e:chat`                  |
| Desktop fork/rewind smoke   | `pnpm run smoke:desktop:fork-rewind` |
| Desktop performance smoke   | `pnpm run perf:desktop`              |
| Extension webapp smoke      | `pnpm run smoke:extension-webapps`   |
| Model gateway smoke         | `pnpm run smoke:model-gateway`       |
| Dictation focused suite     | `pnpm run test:dictation`            |

## Strict QA Evidence Addenda

- 2026-06-26, parent follow-up core file-operations QA reran the remaining visible File Explorer operation gap in an
  isolated Testing app on CDP `9615` with state under `/tmp/neon-pilot-qa/core-fileops-context-parent`. The pre-fix
  probe (`visible-ops-probe-summary.json`, `02-src-right-click.png`, `03-file-right-click.png`, `04-src-hover.png`)
  showed that normal rail rows did not open the documented right-click file-operation menu, and hover controls exposed
  New File/New Folder/Move but not Rename/Delete. Parent fixed the custom rail row path in
  `packages/desktop/ui/src/components/workspace/WorkspaceExplorer.tsx` by adding row context-menu handling, a visible
  Rename prompt backed by `renameWorkspacePath`, and remapping for active/open files after rename; regression coverage
  in `WorkspaceExplorer.test.ts` verifies right-click menu Rename/Delete visibility and the rename callback path.
  Post-fix rebuilt live evidence includes `05-postfix-file-explorer-open.png`, `06-postfix-file-context-menu.png`,
  `07-postfix-rename-dialog.png`, `08-postfix-after-rename.png`, `09-postfix-new-folder-dialog.png`,
  `10-postfix-after-new-folder.png`, `11-postfix-delete-menu.png`, `12-postfix-after-delete.png`,
  `13-postfix-after-reload.png`, and `visible-fileops-postfix-summary.json`. The summary proves the UI menu renamed
  `seed.txt` to `renamed-seed.txt`, removed the old path, created `src/qa-visible-folder` from the visible New Folder
  control, deleted `renamed-seed.txt` through the menu/confirmation, survived reload, and kept every visible viewport
  scan clean of raw `/api`, stack, `file://`, local path, filesystem error, and extension-action wrapper markers.
  Validation passed `pnpm --dir packages/desktop exec vitest run ui/src/components/workspace/WorkspaceExplorer.test.ts --reporter=dot`
  and `pnpm --dir packages/desktop run build:ui`. Remaining file/workspace gaps are native picker/Finder variants and
  the ignored-dir policy decision.
- 2026-06-26, parent core workspace/file-path QA reran `system-files` / File Explorer in an isolated Testing app on CDP
  `9614` with state under `/tmp/neon-pilot-qa/core-files-parent`. The first live pass reproduced a real user-visible

  `22-postfix-notes-detail-open.png`, `23-postfix-notes-autosaved.png`, `24-postfix-after-file-ops-refresh.png`,

  `qa autosave line`, route-level create/rename/move/delete left `renamed.txt` with `created in QA`, removed the

  and `pnpm --dir packages/desktop run build:ui`. Remaining workspace/file gaps are native picker/Finder variants,
  ignored-dir policy, and a fully visible context-menu rerun for create/rename/move/delete after this rail activation fix.

Screenshots: `/tmp/neon-row77-cwd-ui-initial.png`, `/tmp/neon-row77-cwd-ui-editor-open.png`, `/tmp/neon-row77-cwd-ui-cancel.png`, `/tmp/neon-row77-cwd-ui-missing.png`, `/tmp/neon-row77-cwd-ui-target.png`, `/tmp/neon-row77-cwd-ui-reload.png`. Focused extension regression coverage confirms `change_working_directory` honors explicit target conversation ids from the admin/CLI path and resolves relative cwd values from the target conversation's cwd; live QA then found `ensure_live` could report success while `change_working_directory` still returned `session_not_live` because the tool used a direct stateful backend API import instead of the host conversation capability. This exposed and fixed a no-workspace UI bug: opening the existing-conversation cwd editor displayed the first saved workspace path even though the conversation was actually neutral chat, creating an accidental switch trap. The fix adds an explicit selected `Chat` option for neutral conversations and avoids passing the neutral backing cwd as the folder-picker default. Regression coverage added `ConversationComposerMeta` neutral-editor rendering and updated the inline-bash refresh assertion to compare refresh deltas; focused tests `pnpm exec vitest run packages/desktop/ui/src/components/conversation/ConversationComposerMeta.test.tsx packages/desktop/ui/src/pages/ConversationPage.lazyComposerMetadata.test.tsx` passed (28 tests), `pnpm --dir packages/desktop run build:ui` passed, and the Testing app was relaunched. Live rerun showed the editor value as `Chat`, cancel restored `Chat - no workspace`, and renderer API evidence showed the backing cwd remained the neutral chat workspace with no git metadata. Evidence: `/tmp/neon-row77-final-cwd/editor-open.png`, `/tmp/neon-row77-final-cwd/choose-folder-stuck.png`, `/tmp/neon-row77-final-cwd/rebuilt-chat-selected-editor.png`, and `/tmp/neon-row77-final-cwd/post-fix-session-context.json`. ## Bundled System Extensions

These are bundled first-party extensions found under `extensions/system-*` with an `extension.json` unless otherwise noted. There is no `installable-extensions/` directory in this checkout. `scripts/default-installable-extensions.mjs` currently marks `system-dynamic-workflows` as a default installable bundle.

### Registered Extension Directory Exception

`extensions/system-neon-pilot-agent` exists in this checkout only as a `dist/` directory without a source `extension.json`, README, or tests. It was not treated as a registered system extension in this inventory. Revisit if packaging discovers it another way.

## Next Pass: Coverage Assignment

For each row above, the next audit should assign:

| Field             | Meaning                                                                             |
| ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Coverage percent  | Estimated feature coverage from automated, smoke, and manual checks.                |
| Coverage type     | Unit, integration, app route, visual, live integration, release manual, or missing. |
| Required QA cases | Concrete scenarios that must pass before the feature is considered covered.         |
| Owner             | Person or agent lane responsible for filling the gap.                               |
| Priority          | P0/P1/P2 based on release risk and user impact.                                     |
